# IrfanView feature inventory (parity reference for Albumen)

> **Provenance note (2026-09-27):** verbatim copy of the IrfanView 4.76 x64 inventory (released 2026-09-18, with the official PlugIns package 4.76) compiled for the Albumen parity plan, rows `IV-0001` to `IV-1880`, under its original title: IrfanView feature inventory (for Albumen parity). The ids are stable and each is placed in exactly one row of [`../albumen-parity.md`](../albumen-parity.md). Do not renumber; a future IrfanView version appends new ids after IV-1880 (see [`../README.md`](../README.md)).

> **Access:** no limits applied. The official 4.76 x64 ZIP was downloaded and its SHA-256 checked against the published value; the bundled help file (140 topics) was decompiled and read in full with the bundled command-line, plug-in, and change notes. 61 rows marked (PK) in their Source column come partly from product knowledge because the help does not spell out every value of those dialogs.

- **Product:** IrfanView 64-bit (i_view64.exe), with the official 64-bit PlugIns package
- **Version:** 4.76, released 2026-09-18 (current as of 2026-09-27). PlugIns package version 4.76 (iview476_plugins_x64_setup.exe, 30.1 MB). Individual PlugIn versions are listed in the PlugIns table.
- **Previous releases used for feature history:** 4.75 (2026-05-15), 4.73 (2025-11-13), 4.72 (2025-05-12), 4.70 (2024-10-11), 4.67 (2024-04-05), 4.65 (2023-12-09), 4.62 (2022-12-09).
- **Author:** Irfan Skiljan. Freeware for non-commercial use.
- **Compiled:** 2026-09-27

## Sources

- Main site: https://www.irfanview.com/ (home, version banner)
- What's new: https://www.irfanview.com/main_history.htm (4.76 changes)
- Full history: https://www.irfanview.com/history_old.htm (cited as the history source; the same text ships as i_changes.txt)
- Formats: https://www.irfanview.com/main_formats.htm
- PlugIns: https://www.irfanview.com/plugins.htm
- 64-bit page: https://www.irfanview.com/64bit.htm
- FAQ: https://www.irfanview.com/faq.htm
- Languages: https://www.irfanview.com/languages.htm ; Skins: https://www.irfanview.com/skins.htm
- Help file: the official online mirror of the bundled CHM, https://www.irfanview.net/help/guida/<topic>.htm (for example hlp_hotkeys.htm, hlp_command_line.htm, hlp_batch_conversion.htm)
- Distribution: https://www.irfanview.info/files/iview476_x64.zip (SHA-256 37f10e9c...33a9eb, matches the value on the 64-bit page)

## Method notes

- I downloaded the official 4.76 x64 ZIP and checked its SHA-256 against the published value. I decompiled the bundled i_view32.chm (the help file shared by both builds, 140 topics) with hh.exe -decompile, and read every topic. I also read the bundled i_options.txt (command line), i_plugins.txt and i_changes.txt. The site pages above were fetched and converted to text.
- The Source column cites the online help mirror page for each help topic. Two topics exist in the 4.76 CHM but not yet on the online mirror: edit_or_set_alpha_transparency and use_inverted_selection. Rows for those cite the history page, which records the 4.70 and 4.65 additions.
- Some rows were generated by script from official lists: formats (Formats page table), PlugIns (PlugIns page), command line switches (i_options.txt), hotkeys (help Hotkeys page), and placeholders (help Text/Pattern page, HTML export page).
- **Rows marked (PK) in the Source column come partly from product knowledge.** The official help does not spell out every value in those dialogs. Examples are the exact resample filter list beyond Lanczos, the batch rename replace and case options, PDF/WebP/JXL/TGA/BMP/ECW/RAW save option details, the contact sheet grid fields, and the effect names Solarize, Swirl, Twirl, Cylinder, Metallic, Radial/Zoom/Motion blur, Rock, Find edges and Noise (partly confirmed by https://www.plaatjes-bewerken.nl/irfanview/irfanview-afbeeldingen/irfanview-effecten). Verify these in the running program before treating them as spec.
- Category rules: `automation` = command line, batch, placeholders and INI deployment. `video` = audio and video playback and export. `print` = printing. `format` = file format support and format specific options. `ai` = ML based (Tesseract LSTM OCR, FaceDetect). QR/barcode reading (ZXing) is `core`. Everything else is `core`.
- Hotkey key names in the help use Wingdings arrows. They are rendered here as Up/Down/Left/Right arrow.
- Text rules: pipe characters inside fields are written as the word "pipe". Fields contain no em or en dashes.
